One caveat first: `/app/.harbor_assets/recording.mp4` doesn't exist in the container (the directory is empty), so I couldn't watch the video. I found the bugs by reading the node-drawing code, and I haven't seen the result in a running client.\n\n**1. Mesh nodes turned by the wrong angle** (`src/client/mesh.cpp:271`)\nThe shared mesh-rotation helper converted the angle with `radToDeg` when it needed `degToRad`. So any mesh node placed facing a direction, mounted on a wall, or turned in small steps was rotated by a meaningless angle. A 90° turn, for example, became about 5157°. Only unrotated meshes looked right.\n\n**2. Resized blocks and leaves drawn in the wrong place** (`src/client/content_mapblock.cpp:381`)\nFor nodes drawn smaller or larger than normal (such as the half- and double-size test nodes), the code moved the box to the node's world position *before* resizing it. The resize then also scaled that position, so the node shifted by an amount that grew with its distance from the world origin. It now resizes around the node's own center first and then moves it into place, which is the correct order.\n\n**3. Ordinary plants drawn upside-down** (`src/mapnode.cpp:164`)\nFor a plain plant that isn't set up for wall mounting, the code reported it as hanging from the ceiling instead of standing on the floor. The plant drawing code then flipped it onto the ceiling of its own node. Plain plants now count as floor-mounted, as signs, torches and rooted plants already did. Plants set up for wall mounting still use their stored direction. I checked every place that reads this value, and only the drawing code for those node types uses it.\n\n**How I checked:**\n- I computed every possible facing for meshes, node boxes and textures and confirmed they agree. I also checked the wall-mount directions and the torch, sign, plant, rail and liquid geometry. None of those had problems.\n- The client builds, and the existing unit tests pass (244 tests, 0 failures). None of those tests cover node rendering, so they don't test these fixes.\n\nThe changes are uncommitted.
